import crypto from 'node:crypto';
export function instantiatePreset(preset,project,options={}){
 const mode=options.mode??'manual';if(!['manual','command'].includes(mode))throw Error('Prüfmodus ungültig');
 if(!project||!project.target?.trim()||!project.candidate?.trim())throw Error('Testumgebung braucht Ziel und Kandidatenidentität. Unter Projekte & Einstellungen ergänzen.');
 const suffix=crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8),base=preset.id.toLowerCase()+'-'+suffix;
 const cases=[{id:'positive',name:'Erfolgsfall',steps:[preset.positive,'Unabhängigen Sollwert vor der Ausführung festhalten.','Tatsächlichen Einstieg und Zielzustand beobachten; verwendete Fixtures benennen.'],expected:preset.oracle},{id:'negative',name:'Gegenprobe',steps:[preset.negative,'Nur einen isolierten Testbestand gezielt verletzen; Produktbestand nicht unkontrolliert ändern.','Prüfen, dass der konkrete fehlerhafte Fall erkannt wird; Ergebnis und unveränderten Fremdbestand lesen.'],expected:'Die definierte Vertragsverletzung wird durch den zugehörigen Test erkannt; kein Erfolg wegen fehlender Ausführung.'},{id:'readback',name:'Wirkung & Zuordnung',steps:['Ziel '+project.target+' und Kandidat '+project.candidate+' anhand unabhängiger Quelle prüfen.','Ergebnis nach dem vorgesehenen Neustart, Readback oder erneutem Einstieg verifizieren.',preset.scope],expected:'Der tatsächliche Zustand stimmt mit dem unabhängigen Soll überein. Test-ID, Ziel, Kandidat und Aussagegrenze sind eindeutig dokumentiert.'}];
 const variants=options.variants??preset.variants??['Standard'];if(!Array.isArray(variants)||!variants.length||variants.length>10||variants.some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||!v.trim())||new Set(variants).size!==variants.length)throw Error('1–10 eindeutige Varianten erforderlich');
 const tests=variants.flatMap((variant,n)=>cases.map(item=>{let config;const expected='Variante '+variant+': '+item.expected;if(mode==='manual')config={steps:['Variante '+variant+' unabhängig von Nachbarfällen prüfen.',...item.steps],expected};else{const command=options.commands?.[item.id];if(!command?.executable||!Array.isArray(command.args)||!Array.isArray(command.expectedExitCodes))throw Error('Für jeden Fall Programm, Argumentliste und erwartete Exitcodes konfigurieren');config={...command,args:command.args.map(a=>a.replaceAll('{{variant}}',variant))}}
 return {id:base+'-v'+(n+1)+'-'+item.id,name:`${preset.id} · ${preset.name} · ${variant} · ${item.name}`,purpose:preset.purpose,oracle:expected,counterexamples:preset.negative,scope:preset.scope,source:preset.source,type:mode,category:preset.category,projectId:project.id,tags:[preset.id,preset.group,variant,item.id],enabled:true,timeoutMs:options.timeoutMs??60000,config}}));
 return {tests,suite:{id:base,name:options.name??`${preset.id} · ${preset.name}`,testIds:tests.map(t=>t.id),stopOnFailure:false}};
}

export function reviewResult(result,test,input){
 const {status,note,evidence,reviewer,steps}=input;
 const requiredText=v=>typeof v==='string'&&v.trim().length>0;
 if(!requiredText(reviewer))throw Error('Prüfer erforderlich');
 const at=new Date().toISOString();
 if(test.type==='checklist'){
  const original=result.evidence.steps??test.config.steps;
  const pending=original.filter(s=>s.required&&s.status==='pending');
  if(!Array.isArray(steps)||steps.length!==pending.length||new Set(steps.map(s=>s.id)).size!==steps.length||steps.some(s=>!pending.some(p=>p.id===s.id)))throw Error('Für jeden offenen Pflichtschritt ein eigenes Urteil erforderlich');
  for(const s of steps)if(!['passed','failed'].includes(s.status)||!requiredText(s.note)||!requiredText(s.evidence))throw Error('Schritturteil, Beobachtung und Nachweisreferenz erforderlich');
  const reviewed=original.map(s=>{const r=steps.find(r=>r.id===s.id);return r?{...s,status:r.status,note:r.note,evidence:r.evidence,reviewer,at,evidenceClass:'human-attestation'}:s});
  const required=reviewed.filter(s=>s.required);
  result.status=required.some(s=>s.status==='failed')?'failed':required.some(s=>s.status==='pending')?'pending_manual':'passed';
  result.evidence={...result.evidence,steps:reviewed,review:{reviewer,at,evidenceClass:'human-attestation'}};
  result.assertions=required.map(s=>({label:s.label,passed:s.status==='passed',actual:s.status,expected:'passed'}));
 }else{
  if(!['passed','failed'].includes(status)||!requiredText(note)||!requiredText(evidence))throw Error('Urteil, Prüfer, Beobachtung und Nachweisreferenz erforderlich');
  result.status=status;
  result.evidence.review={status,note,evidence,reviewer,at,evidenceClass:'human-attestation'};
  result.assertions=[{label:'Manuelles Urteil',passed:status==='passed',actual:note,expected:test.config.expected}];
 }
 return result;
}

export function atomic(file,value){fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file),{recursive:true});const tmp=file+'.'+crypto.randomUUID()+'.tmp';fs.writeFileSync(tmp,JSON.stringify(value,null,2),'utf8');fs.renameSync(tmp,file)}
export function aggregate(results){const states=results.map(r=>r.status);if(states.includes('running')||states.includes('queued'))return 'running';for(const s of ['cancelled','interrupted','error','timeout','failed','pending_manual','skipped'])if(states.includes(s))return s;return states.length&&states.every(s=>s==='passed')?'passed':'error'}
export async function killTree(child){
 if(!child.pid)return;
 if(process.platform==='win32'){const exe=path.join(process.env.SystemRoot??'C:/Windows','System32','taskkill.exe');await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const p=spawn(exe,['/PID',String(child.pid),'/T','/F'],{windowsHide:true,stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe']});let error='';p.stderr.on('data',b=>error+=b);p.on('error',reject);p.on('close',code=>{if(code!==0&&child.exitCode===null)reject(Error('Prozessbaum-Abbruch nicht bestätigt: '+error));else resolve()})})}
 else {try{process.kill(-child.pid,'SIGKILL')}catch(e){if(e.code!=='ESRCH')throw e}await verifyPosixTermination(child)}
}
export async function runOne(test,project,settings,signal,{terminate=killTree}={}){
 const startedAt=new Date().toISOString(),start=performance.now();
 if(test.enabled===false)return {testId:test.id,status:'skipped',reason:'Test deaktiviert',startedAt,durationMs:0,assertions:[],evidence:{}};
 if(signal?.aborted)return {testId:test.id,status:'cancelled',startedAt,durationMs:0,assertions:[],evidence:{}};
 if(test.type==='manual')return {testId:test.id,status:'pending_manual',startedAt,durationMs:0,assertions:[],evidence:{steps:test.config.steps,expected:test.config.expected}};
 return new Promise(resolve=>{
 const child=fork(path.join(here,'worker.mjs'),[],{stdio:['ignore','ignore','pipe','ipc'],windowsHide:true,detached:process.platform!=='win32'});let done=false,timer,stderr='';const stderrDecoder=new StringDecoder('utf8');
 const finish=async(result,kill=false)=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearTimeout(timer);signal?.removeEventListener('abort',cancel);if(kill)try{await terminate(child);result.evidence={...result.evidence,termination:process.platform==='win32'?'process-tree-terminated':'process-group-terminated',terminationScope:process.platform==='win32'?'taskkill-tree':'worker-process-group; detached groups excluded'}}catch(e){result={status:'error',error:e.message,evidence:{requestedStatus:result.status,termination:'unverified'},assertions:[]}}resolve({testId:test.id,startedAt,finishedAt:new Date().toISOString(),durationMs:performance.now()-start,assertions:[],evidence:{},...result})};
 const cancel=()=>finish({status:'cancelled',reason:'Vom Nutzer abgebrochen'},true);
 child.stderr.on('data',b=>{stderr=(stderr+stderrDecoder.write(b)).slice(-4000)});child.once('message',r=>finish(r,true));child.once('error',e=>finish({status:'error',error:e.message}));child.once('exit',(code,signalName)=>{if(!done)finish({status:'error',error:`Adapter ohne Ergebnis beendet (${code}/${signalName})`,evidence:{stderr}})});
 timer=setTimeout(()=>finish({status:'timeout',reason:`Zeitlimit ${test.timeoutMs} ms überschritten`},true),test.timeoutMs);signal?.addEventListener('abort',cancel,{once:true});if(signal?.aborted)cancel();else try{child.send({test,project,settings},error=>{if(error)void finish({status:'error',error:error.message},true)})}catch(error){void finish({status:'error',error:error.message},true)};
 });
}
export class Store{
 constructor(dir,initial){this.dir=dir;fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir,'runs'),{recursive:true});this.libraryFile=path.join(dir,'library.json');this.warnings=[];this.library=recoverLibrary(this.libraryFile,initial,this.warnings,atomic);this.runs=new Map();for(const name of fs.readdirSync(path.join(dir,'runs'))){if(!name.endsWith('.json'))continue;try{const run=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,'runs',name),'utf8'));if(!Array.isArray(run.results)||!run.id)throw Error('Ungültiger Lauf');if(run.status==='running'){for(const r of run.results)if(['running','queued'].includes(r.status)){r.status='interrupted';r.reason='Dienst während Lauf beendet'}run.status=aggregate(run.results);run.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();this.saveRun(run)}this.runs.set(run.id,run)}catch(e){this.warnings.push(`${name}: ${e.message}`)}}}
 saveLibrary(library){validateLibrary(library);const backup=path.join(this.dir,'backups',Date.now()+'-'+crypto.randomUUID()+'.json');atomic(backup,this.library);atomic(this.libraryFile,library);this.library=structuredClone(library)}
 saveRun(run){atomic(path.join(this.dir,'runs',run.id+'.json'),run);this.runs?.set(run.id,run)}
 listRuns(){return [...this.runs.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.startedAt.localeCompare(a.startedAt)).map(({results,definition,...r})=>({...r,summary:results.reduce((a,r)=>(a[r.status]=(a[r.status]??0)+1,a),{})}))}
}
export class Runner{
 constructor(store,{executeOne=runOne}={}){this.store=store;this.executeOne=executeOne;this.active=null;this.engineHash=engineIdentity()}
 start({testIds,suiteId,repeat=1,stopOnFailure=false,requestId}){
 const requestHash=hash({testIds,suiteId,repeat,stopOnFailure});
 if(requestId){const previous=[...this.store.runs.values()].find(r=>r.requestId===requestId);if(previous){if(previous.requestHash!==requestHash)throw Error('requestId wurde bereits für eine andere Anforderung verwendet');return previous}}
 if(this.active)throw Error('Es läuft bereits ein Testplan');
 if(engineIdentity()!==this.engineHash)throw Error('Engine-Dateien wurden geändert. Dienst vor dem nächsten Lauf neu starten.');
 const library=structuredClone(this.store.library);let suite;if(suiteId){suite=library.suites.find(s=>s.id===suiteId);if(!suite)throw Error('Testplan fehlt');testIds=suite.testIds;stopOnFailure=suite.stopOnFailure??false}
 if(!Array.isArray(testIds)||!testIds.length||new Set(testIds).size!==testIds.length||testIds.some(id=>!library.tests.some(t=>t.id===id)))throw Error('Eindeutige vorhandene Test-IDs erforderlich');
 if(!Number.isInteger(repeat)||repeat<1||repeat>100)throw Error('Wiederholungen müssen 1–100 sein');if(testIds.length*repeat>10000)throw Error('Maximal 10000 Ausführungen je Lauf');
 if(typeof stopOnFailure!=='boolean')throw Error('stopOnFailure muss boolean sein');if(requestId!==undefined&&(typeof requestId!=='string'||requestId.length>100))throw Error('requestId ungültig');
 const definition={projects:library.projects,tests:testIds.map(id=>library.tests.find(t=>t.id===id)),suite,settings:library.settings??{allowCommands:false},repeat,stopOnFailure};
 const run={id:crypto.randomUUID(),requestId:requestId??crypto.randomUUID(),requestHash,name:suite?.name??'Gezielte Auswahl',status:'running',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),engineVersion:VERSION,engineHash:this.engineHash,definitionHash:hash(definition),definition,results:[]};
 for(let i=1;i<=repeat;i++)for(const t of definition.tests)run.results.push({testId:t.id,iteration:i,status:'queued',assertions:[],evidence:{}});
 this.store.saveRun(run);const controller=new AbortController();this.active={id:run.id,controller};this.completion=this.execute(run,controller).finally(()=>{this.active=null});return run;
 }
 async execute(run,controller){let stopped=false;try{for(let i=0;i<run.results.length;i++){const slot=run.results[i];if(controller.signal.aborted||stopped){slot.status=controller.signal.aborted?'cancelled':'skipped';slot.reason=controller.signal.aborted?'Lauf abgebrochen':'Testplan nach Fehler gestoppt';this.store.saveRun(run);continue}slot.status='running';this.store.saveRun(run);const test=run.definition.tests.find(t=>t.id===slot.testId),project=run.definition.projects.find(p=>p.id===test.projectId);let result;try{result=await this.executeOne(test,project,run.definition.settings,controller.signal)}catch(error){result={testId:test.id,status:'error',error:error.message,assertions:[],evidence:{failureScope:'adapter-invocation'}}}run.results[i]={...result,iteration:slot.iteration};if(run.definition.stopOnFailure&&['failed','error','timeout'].includes(result.status))stopped=true;this.store.saveRun(run)}run.status=aggregate(run.results)}catch(e){run.status='error';run.error=e.message;for(const r of run.results)if(['running','queued'].includes(r.status)){r.status='error';r.error='Lauf-Infrastrukturfehler'}}run.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();this.store.saveRun(run);return run}
 cancel(id){if(this.active?.id!==id)throw Error('Lauf ist nicht aktiv');this.active.controller.abort()}
 manual(runId,index,input){const run=this.store.runs.get(runId);if(!run)throw Error('Lauf fehlt');if(run.status==='running')throw Error('Automatische Ausführung zuerst abschließen');const result=run.results[index];if(!result||result.status!=='pending_manual')throw Error('Keine offene manuelle Prüfung');reviewResult(result,run.definition.tests.find(t=>t.id===result.testId),input);run.status=aggregate(run.results);this.store.saveRun(run);return run}
}
const xml=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f]/g,'').replace(/[<>&"']/g,c=>({'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&apos;'}[c]));
const csv=s=>'"'+String(s??'').replace(/^(?:[\t\r\n]|\s*[=+@-])/u,match=>"'"+match).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"';
export function exportRun(run,format){
 if(format==='json')return JSON.stringify(run,null,2);
 if(format==='csv')return ['testId,iteration,status,durationMs,details',...run.results.map(r=>[r.testId,r.iteration,r.status,r.durationMs,r.error??r.reason??JSON.stringify(r.assertions)].map(csv).join(','))].join('\r\n');
 if(format==='junit'){const errors=run.results.filter(r=>['error','timeout','interrupted'].includes(r.status)).length,failures=run.results.filter(r=>r.status==='failed').length;return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><testsuite name="${xml(run.name)}" tests="${run.results.length}" failures="${failures}" errors="${errors}" skipped="${run.results.filter(r=>!['passed','failed','error','timeout','interrupted'].includes(r.status)).length}">${run.results.map(r=>`<testcase name="${xml(r.testId)} #${r.iteration}" time="${(r.durationMs??0)/1000}">${r.status==='passed'?'':r.status==='failed'?`<failure message="Assertion failed">${xml(JSON.stringify(r.assertions))}</failure>`:['error','timeout','interrupted'].includes(r.status)?`<error message="${xml(r.status)}">${xml(r.error??r.reason)}</error>`:`<skipped message="${xml(r.status)}"/>`}<system-out>${xml(JSON.stringify(r.evidence))}</system-out></testcase>`).join('')}</testsuite>`}
 throw Error('Exportformat unbekannt');
}

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
export function acquireLock(dir){
 fs.mkdirSync(dir,{recursive:true});const file=path.join(dir,'service.lock'),identity={pid:process.pid,id:crypto.randomUUID(),at:new Date().toISOString()};
 if(fs.existsSync(file)){
  const prior=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8'));if(!Number.isInteger(prior.pid)||prior.pid<1)throw Error('Ungültige Dienstsperre: '+file);
  let alive=true;try{process.kill(prior.pid,0)}catch(e){if(e.code==='ESRCH')alive=false;else throw e}
  if(alive)throw Error('Datenordner wird bereits von einem Dienst verwendet (PID '+prior.pid+')');
  fs.unlinkSync(file);
 }
 const fd=fs.openSync(file,'wx');try{fs.writeFileSync(fd,JSON.stringify(identity))}finally{fs.closeSync(fd)}
 return ()=>{if(fs.existsSync(file)&&JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8')).id===identity.id)fs.unlinkSync(file)};
}

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
export function sourceFiles(root){
 const names=['package.json','server.mjs','cli.mjs','README.md','CONTRACT.md','VERIFICATION.md','.gitignore','.gitattributes','.github/workflows/test.yml','Start-Evaluator.cmd','Stop-Evaluator.cmd','Start-Evaluator.ps1','Stop-Evaluator.ps1'];
 const walk=dir=>{for(const e of fs.readdirSync(path.join(root,dir),{withFileTypes:true})){const n=dir+'/'+e.name;if(e.isDirectory())walk(n);else if(/\.(mjs|js|html|css|json|md|txt|csv)$/.test(e.name))names.push(n)}};
 for(const dir of ['lib','public','resources','tests','examples'])walk(dir);
 for(const n of ['verify.mjs','export-examples.mjs','package.mjs','verify-package.ps1'])names.push('tools/'+n);
 return names.filter(n=>fs.existsSync(path.join(root,n))).sort().map(name=>{const data=fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,name));return {name,data,sha256:crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex')}});
}
export function sourceText(root){const files=sourceFiles(root);return 'Evaluator – Inspector · vollständiger Produktquelltext\nDateien: '+files.length+'\nUTF-8 · Quelldateien mit relativen Pfaden; keine Nutzerdaten oder gespeicherten Läufe.\n\n'+files.map(f=>'='.repeat(78)+'\nFILE: '+f.name+'\nSHA256: '+f.sha256+'\n'+'='.repeat(78)+'\n'+f.data.toString('utf8')).join('\n\n')}

function runTable(list){if(!list.length)return '<div class="empty"><b>Noch keine Testläufe</b>Starte einen Test oder den mitgelieferten Beispielplan.</div>';return `<div class="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>Testlauf</th><th>Zeitpunkt</th><th>Ergebnis</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${list.map(r=>`<tr><td><strong>${esc(r.name)}</strong><small>${esc(r.id.slice(0,8))} · ${Object.values(r.summary).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)} Prüfungen</small></td><td>${date(r.startedAt)}</td><td>${badge(r.status)}</td><td><button class="text-button" data-run="${r.id}">Öffnen →</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`}
function tests(){const filter=$('#search')?.value??'',type=$('#typeFilter')?.value??'',cat=$('#categoryFilter')?.value??'';$('#content').innerHTML=head('Testkatalog','Jeder Test hat einen Zweck, einen Prüfadapter und überprüfbare Sollwerte.',`<button class="secondary" data-action="export-library">Exportieren</button><button class="primary" data-action="new">＋ Test erstellen</button>`)+`<div class="toolbar"><input id="search" placeholder="Tests durchsuchen …" aria-label="Tests durchsuchen" value="${esc(filter)}"><select id="typeFilter" aria-label="Adapter filtern"><option value="">Alle Adapter</option>${Object.entries(typeNames).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}" ${type===k?'selected':''}>${v}</option>`).join('')}</select><select id="categoryFilter" aria-label="Kategorie filtern"><option value="">Alle Kategorien</option>${[...new Set(state.library.tests.map(t=>t.category))].map(c=>`<option ${cat===c?'selected':''}>${esc(c)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div class="actions"><button class="primary" data-action="run-selected" ${state.active||!selected.size?'disabled':''}>▷ Auswahl ausführen</button><button class="secondary" data-action="suite-selected">Als Testplan speichern</button><label>Wiederholungen <input id="repeat" type="number" min="1" max="100" value="1" class="inline-number"></label></div><span class="count-label" id="selectedCount">${selected.size} ausgewählt</span></div><div class="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><input type="checkbox" id="selectAll" aria-label="Sichtbare Tests auswählen"></th><th>Test / Zweck</th><th>Adapter</th><th>Kategorie</th><th>Status</th><th>Aktionen</th></tr></thead><tbody id="testRows"></tbody></table></div></div><p class="hint">Wiederholungen sind ausdrücklich geplante Ausführungen. Fehlschläge werden nicht automatisch verdeckt oder erneut versucht.</p>`;testRows()}
function visibleTests(){const q=($('#search')?.value??'').toLowerCase(),type=$('#typeFilter')?.value,cat=$('#categoryFilter')?.value;return state.library.tests.filter(t=>(!type||t.type===type)&&(!cat||t.category===cat)&&[t.name,t.purpose,t.id,...t.tags??[]].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))}
function testRows(){const ts=visibleTests();$('#testRows').innerHTML=ts.map(t=>`<tr><td><input type="checkbox" data-select="${t.id}" ${selected.has(t.id)?'checked':''} aria-label="${esc(t.name)} auswählen"></td><td><strong>${esc(t.name)}</strong><small>${esc(t.purpose)}</small><small class="mono">${esc(t.id)} · ${esc(t.projectId)}</small></td><td><span class="type">${esc(t.type.toUpperCase())}</span></td><td>${esc(t.category)}</td><td><span class="badge">${t.enabled===false?'Deaktiviert':'Definiert'}</span></td><td><div class="actions"><button class="secondary" data-edit="${t.id}">Bearbeiten</button><button class="primary" data-start="${t.id}" ${state.active?'disabled':''}>▷</button><button class="secondary" data-duplicate="${t.id}" title="Duplizieren">⧉</button><button class="danger" data-delete="${t.id}" title="Entfernen">×</button></div></td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">Keine Tests für diesen Filter.</td></tr>'}
function suites(){$('#content').innerHTML=head('Testpläne','Stelle aus vorhandenen Tests wiederholbare Prüfabläufe zusammen.',`<button class="primary" data-action="new-suite">＋ Testplan erstellen</button>`)+`<div class="suite-grid">${state.library.suites.map(s=>`<div class="suite-card"><div class="eyebrow">TESTPLAN · ${s.testIds.length} TESTS</div><h2>${esc(s.name)}</h2><p>${s.testIds.map(id=>esc(state.library.tests.find(t=>t.id===id)?.name)).join(' · ')}</p><span class="badge">${s.stopOnFailure?'Stoppt nach Fehler':'Führt alle Tests aus'}</span> ${badge(state.gates?.[s.id]?.status??'untested')}<p class="hint">${esc(state.gates?.[s.id]?.reason??'Noch kein vollständiger Testplanlauf')}</p><div class="actions"><button class="primary" data-suite-run="${s.id}" ${state.active?'disabled':''}>▷ Testplan ausführen</button><button class="secondary" data-suite-edit="${s.id}">Bearbeiten</button><button class="danger" data-suite-delete="${s.id}">Entfernen</button></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty"><b>Noch kein Testplan</b>Wähle Tests aus dem Katalog oder erstelle hier einen Plan.</div>'}</div>`}
function files(){const pid=$('#fileProject')?.value??state.library.projects[0]?.id;$('#content').innerHTML=head('Dateien & Abdeckung','Inventar der registrierten Projektordner. Zugeordnete Tests sind keine automatische Aussage über vollständige Testabdeckung.')+`<div class="toolbar"><select id="fileProject" aria-label="Projekt für Inventar">${state.library.projects.map(p=>`<option value="${p.id}" ${pid===p.id?'selected':''}>${esc(p.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select><button class="primary" data-action="scan">Dateien erfassen</button><input id="fileSearch" placeholder="Dateipfad filtern …" aria-label="Dateipfad filtern"></div><div id="inventoryInfo" class="hint"></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>Datei / Ordner</th><th>Typ</th><th>Größe</th><th>Direkte Testzuordnung</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody id="fileRows"></tbody></table></div></div>`;fileRows()}
function fileRows(){if(!fileData||fileData.projectId!==$('#fileProject').value){$('#fileRows').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Projekt wählen und Dateien erfassen.</td></tr>';return}const {files,errors,truncated,limit,excludedDirectories}=fileData;$('#inventoryInfo').textContent=`${files.length} Einträge · ${errors.length} Lesefehler${truncated?' · UNVOLLSTÄNDIG: Limit '+limit+' erreicht':''}. Ausgelassen: ${excludedDirectories.join(', ')}; Verknüpfungen werden nicht verfolgt.`;const normalize=s=>s.replaceAll(String.fromCharCode(92),'/').toLowerCase(),q=normalize($('#fileSearch').value);$('#fileRows').innerHTML=files.filter(f=>normalize(f.path).includes(q)).map(f=>`<tr><td class="file-path">${esc(f.path)}</td><td>${esc(f.kind)}</td><td>${f.bytes??'—'}</td><td>${f.testIds.length?f.testIds.map(id=>`<span class="badge">${esc(id)}</span>`).join(' '):'<span class="note">Keine direkte Zuordnung</span>'}</td><td>${f.kind==='file'?`<button class="text-button" data-file="${esc(f.path)}">＋ Test</button>`:''}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Keine Dateien für diesen Filter.</td></tr>';if(errors.length)$('#inventoryInfo').textContent+=' Fehler: '+errors.map(e=>e.path+': '+e.error).join('; ')}
function runs(){if(currentRun){runDetail();return}const list=state.runs;$('#content').innerHTML=head('Läufe & Auswertung','Ergebniszustände, Prüfregeln und Nachweise jedes Laufs bleiben erhalten.')+`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h2>Verlauf der letzten ${Math.min(list.length,20)} Läufe</h2><span class="count-label">Säulenhöhe: Anzahl Prüfungen</span></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="trend">${list.slice(0,20).reverse().map(r=>`<button data-run="${r.id}" class="${r.status==='passed'?'':r.status==='pending_manual'?'open':'bad'}" style="height:80%" title="${esc(date(r.startedAt)+' · '+statuses[r.status])}">${Object.values(r.summary).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="trend-label"><span>Älter</span><span>Neuester Lauf →</span></div></div></div><div class="panel">${runTable(list)}</div>`;const bars=$('.trend')?.children??[],max=Math.max(1,...list.slice(0,20).map(r=>Object.values(r.summary).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)));[...bars].forEach((b,i)=>b.style.height=Math.max(20,Object.values(list.slice(0,20).reverse()[i].summary).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/max*100)+'%')}
function runDetail(){const r=currentRun;$('#content').innerHTML=head(esc(r.name),`${date(r.startedAt)} · ${r.id}`,`<button class="secondary" data-action="back-runs">← Alle Läufe</button>${state.active===r.id?`<button class="danger" data-cancel="${r.id}">Lauf abbrechen</button>`:''}`)+`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div class="actions">${badge(r.status)}<span class="count-label">${r.results.length} Prüfungen · Definition ${r.definitionHash.slice(0,12)}</span></div><div class="actions">${['json','csv','junit'].map(f=>`<button class="secondary" data-export="${f}">${f.toUpperCase()} ↓</button>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="panel-body"><div class="toolbar"><label>Mit vorherigem Lauf vergleichen<select id="compareRun"><option value="">Vergleichslauf wählen</option>${state.runs.filter(x=>x.id!==r.id&&x.status!=='running').map(x=>`<option value="${x.id}">${date(x.startedAt)} · ${esc(x.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label></div><div id="comparison"></div><p class="hint">Engine ${r.engineVersion} · Quellidentität ${r.engineHash.slice(0,16)} · ${r.finishedAt?'Beendet '+date(r.finishedAt):'Ausführung läuft'}</p>${r.error?`<p class="error">${esc(r.error)}</p>`:''}${r.results.map((result,i)=>{const t=r.definition.tests.find(t=>t.id===result.testId);return `<details class="run-result" ${['failed','error','timeout','pending_manual'].includes(result.status)?'open':''}><summary><strong>${esc(t.name)}</strong><span class="note">#${result.iteration} · ${duration(result.durationMs)}</span>${badge(result.status)}</summary><div class="result-body"><p class="note">${esc(t.purpose)}</p><p class="hint"><strong>Umgebung:</strong> ${esc(r.definition.projects.find(p=>p.id===t.projectId)?.target??t.projectId)} · <strong>Kandidat:</strong> ${esc(r.definition.projects.find(p=>p.id===t.projectId)?.candidate??'Nicht deklariert')}</p>${t.oracle?`<p class="note"><strong>Sollkriterium:</strong> ${esc(t.oracle)}</p>`:''}${t.counterexamples?`<p class="note"><strong>Gegenfälle:</strong> ${esc(t.counterexamples)}</p>`:''}${t.scope?`<p class="hint"><strong>Aussagegrenze:</strong> ${esc(t.scope)}</p>`:''}${result.error||result.reason?`<p class="error">${esc(result.error??result.reason)}</p>`:''}${(result.assertions??[]).map(a=>`<div class="assertion"><strong>${a.passed?'✓':'×'}</strong>${esc(a.label)}<small>Ist: ${esc(JSON.stringify(a.actual))}<br>Soll: ${esc(JSON.stringify(a.expected))}</small></div>`).join('')}${result.status==='pending_manual'?`<ol>${t.config.steps.map(s=>`<li>${esc(typeof s==='string'?s:s.label+' · '+(result.evidence.steps?.find(x=>x.id===s.id)?.status??'pending'))}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p>Soll: ${esc(t.config.expected??t.oracle??'Alle Pflichtschritte mit Nachweisen abschließen')}</p><button class="primary" data-manual="${i}">Manuelles Urteil erfassen</button>`:''}<details><summary>Nachweise & Messwerte</summary><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(result.evidence,null,2))}</pre></details><details><summary>Ausgeführte Testdefinition</summary><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(t,null,2))}</pre></details></div></details>`}).join('')}</div></div>`}
function settings(){$('#content').innerHTML=head('Projekte & Einstellungen','Projektordner bestimmen den Bereich für Datei- und Datenprüfungen.',`<button class="primary" data-action="new-project">＋ Projekt hinzufügen</button>`)+`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h2>Registrierte Projekte</h2></div><table><thead><tr><th>Projekt</th><th>Lokaler Ordner</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${state.library.projects.map(p=>`<tr><td><strong>${esc(p.name)}</strong><small>${p.id}</small></td><td class="file-path">${esc(p.root)}<small>Ziel: ${esc(p.target??'Noch nicht deklariert')} · Kandidat: ${esc(p.candidate??'Noch nicht deklariert')}</small></td><td><button class="secondary" data-project="${p.id}">Bearbeiten</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h2>Externe Testprogramme</h2></div><div class="panel-body"><label class="check"><input id="allowCommands" type="checkbox" ${state.library.settings?.allowCommands?'checked':''}> Ausführung lokaler Programme erlauben</label><p class="hint">Programmtests führen die angegebene ausführbare Datei mit ihrer Argumentliste aus. Programme arbeiten mit deinen lokalen Benutzerrechten und können Dateien oder Dienste verändern. Prüfe importierte Befehle vor dem Start.</p></div></div><div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><h2>Schnittstelle für Nutzer und externe KI</h2></div><div class="panel-body"><p class="note">Versionierte JSON-Bibliotheken enthalten Projekte, Tests und Testpläne. Importierte Definitionen werden vor der Übernahme validiert. Die Ausführung benötigt keine KI.</p><div class="actions"><button class="secondary" data-action="export-library">Bibliothek exportieren</button><button class="secondary" data-action="import">Bibliothek importieren</button><button class="secondary" data-action="api-help">API & CLI anzeigen</button><button class="secondary" data-action="source-code">Kompletter Code (.txt)</button></div><p class="hint">Bibliotheksänderungen erhalten eine lokale Sicherung. Laufnachweise liegen in .data/runs; Projektdateien werden vom Cockpit nicht bearbeitet.</p></div></div>${state.warnings.length?`<div class="warning">Historienwarnungen: ${esc(state.warnings.join('; '))}</div>`:''}`}
function editor(test=null,{file,projectId,duplicate=false}={}){editingId=duplicate?null:test?.id??null;$('#editorTitle').textContent=editingId?'Test bearbeiten':'Test erstellen';$('#tType').innerHTML=Object.entries(typeNames).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}">${v}</option>`).join('');$('#tProject').innerHTML=state.library.projects.map(p=>`<option value="${p.id}">${esc(p.name)}</option>`).join('');$('#categories').innerHTML=state.categories.map(c=>`<option value="${esc(c)}">`).join('');$('#tId').value=duplicate?test.id+'-copy':test?.id??'test-'+crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8);$('#tId').readOnly=!!editingId;$('#tName').value=duplicate?test.name+' (Kopie)':test?.name??(file?'Datei prüfen: '+file:'');$('#tPurpose').value=test?.purpose??'';$('#tType').value=test?.type??'file';$('#tProject').value=projectId??test?.projectId??state.library.projects[0]?.id;$('#tCategory').value=test?.category??'Regression';$('#tTimeout').value=test?.timeoutMs??10000;$('#tTags').value=(test?.tags??[]).join(', ');$('#tOracle').value=test?.oracle??'';$('#tCounterexamples').value=test?.counterexamples??'';$('#tScope').value=test?.scope??'';$('#tSource').value=test?.source??'';$('#tEnabled').checked=test?.enabled!==false;$('#tConfig').value=JSON.stringify(test?.config??(file?{path:file,exists:true,kind:'file'}:state.templates.file),null,2);$('#configHint').textContent=hints[$('#tType').value];$('#editorError').textContent='';$('#editor').showModal();if(conflictPanel)conflictHost().prepend(conflictPanel)}
function generic(title,html){$('#genericTitle').textContent=title;$('#genericBody').innerHTML=html;$('#generic').showModal();if(conflictPanel)conflictHost().prepend(conflictPanel)}
function suiteEditor(s){generic(s?'Testplan bearbeiten':'Testplan erstellen',`<form id="suiteForm"><label>Name<input id="sName" required value="${esc(s?.name??'')}"></label><label>Stabile ID<input id="sId" required pattern="[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,80}" value="${s?.id??'plan-'+crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8)}" ${s?'readonly':''}></label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" id="sStop" ${s?.stopOnFailure?'checked':''}> Nach erstem Fehler stoppen</label><div class="checkbox-list">${state.library.tests.map(t=>`<label><input type="checkbox" name="suiteTest" value="${t.id}" ${(s?.testIds??[...selected]).includes(t.id)?'checked':''}>${esc(t.name)} <span class="type">${t.type}</span></label>`).join('')}</div><div class="dialog-actions"><button class="primary">Testplan speichern</button></div></form>`);$('#suiteForm').onsubmit=guard(async e=>{e.preventDefault();const plan={id:$('#sId').value,name:$('#sName').value,testIds:[...document.querySelectorAll('[name=suiteTest]:checked')].map(x=>x.value),stopOnFailure:$('#sStop').checked};const l=structuredClone(state.library);if(!s&&l.suites.some(x=>x.id===plan.id))throw Error('ID bereits vorhanden');l.suites=l.suites.filter(x=>x.id!==plan.id);l.suites.push(plan);await save(l);$('#generic').close();toast('Testplan gespeichert')})}
async function start(options){if(state.active)throw Error('Ein Lauf ist bereits aktiv');const run=await api('/runs','POST',{...options,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});state.active=run.id;currentRun=run;view='runs';history.replaceState(null,'','#runs/'+run.id);await refresh();render();toast('Testlauf gestartet')}
async function refresh(){const data=await api('/runs');state.runs=data.runs;state.gates=data.gates;state.active=data.active;if(currentRun)currentRun=await api('/runs/'+currentRun.id)}
function download(name,text,type='application/json'){const a=document.createElement('a'),url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type}));a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),5000)}
function guard(fn){return async(...args)=>{try{await fn(...args)}catch(e){toast(e.message,true)}}}
document.addEventListener('click',guard(async e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b||b.disabled)return;const d=b.dataset;if(d.close){$('#'+d.close).close();return}if(d.view){show(d.view);return}if(d.edit){editor(state.library.tests.find(t=>t.id===d.edit));return}if(d.duplicate){editor(state.library.tests.find(t=>t.id===d.duplicate),{duplicate:true});return}if(d.start){await start({testIds:[d.start]});return}if(d.suiteRun){await start({suiteId:d.suiteRun});return}if(d.suiteEdit){suiteEditor(state.library.suites.find(s=>s.id===d.suiteEdit));return}if(d.file){editor(null,{file:d.file,projectId:$('#fileProject').value});return}if(d.run){currentRun=await api('/runs/'+d.run);view='runs';history.replaceState(null,'','#runs/'+d.run);render();return}if(d.cancel){await api('/runs/'+d.cancel+'/cancel','POST',{});toast('Abbruch angefordert; Prozessbeendigung wird geprüft.');return}if(d.export){const response=await fetch(`/api/runs/${currentRun.id}/export?format=${d.export}`,{headers:{'X-Cockpit-Token':state.token}});if(!response.ok)throw Error('Export fehlgeschlagen');download('testlauf-'+currentRun.id+'.'+(d.export==='junit'?'xml':d.export),await response.text());return}
if(d.delete||d.suiteDelete){const id=d.delete??d.suiteDelete;generic('Eintrag entfernen',`<p>Eintrag <strong>${esc(id)}</strong> aus der Bibliothek entfernen? Gespeicherte Laufnachweise bleiben erhalten.</p><div class="dialog-actions"><button class="secondary" data-close="generic">Abbrechen</button><button class="danger" id="confirmDelete">Entfernen</button></div>`);$('#confirmDelete').onclick=guard(async()=>{const l=structuredClone(state.library);if(d.delete){if(l.suites.some(s=>s.testIds.includes(id)))throw Error('Test wird in einem Testplan verwendet. Zuerst den Testplan anpassen.');l.tests=l.tests.filter(t=>t.id!==id);selected.delete(id)}else l.suites=l.suites.filter(s=>s.id!==id);await save(l);$('#generic').close()});return}
if(d.manual!==undefined){const index=Number(d.manual),result=currentRun.results[index],test=currentRun.definition.tests.find(t=>t.id===result.testId),pending=test.type==='checklist'?(result.evidence.steps??test.config.steps).filter(s=>s.required&&s.status==='pending'):null;generic('Manuelles Prüfurteil',`<form id="manualForm"><p class="hint">Dieses Urteil wird als dokumentierte Beobachtung gespeichert. Eine Nachweisreferenz wird nicht automatisch inhaltlich verifiziert. Jedes Urteil bleibt unveränderlich.</p><label>Prüfer<input id="mReviewer" required></label>${pending?pending.map((s,i)=>`<fieldset><legend>${esc(s.label)}</legend><label>Beobachtung<textarea id="mNote-${i}" required></textarea></label><label>Nachweisreferenz<input id="mEvidence-${i}" required></label><label>Schritturteil<select id="mStatus-${i}"><option value="failed">Nicht bestanden</option><option value="passed">Bestanden</option></select></label></fieldset>`).join(''):'<label>Beobachtung<textarea id="mNote" required rows="3"></textarea></label><label>Nachweisreferenz (Dateipfad, Bericht oder Messprotokoll)<input id="mEvidence" required></label><label>Urteil<select id="mStatus"><option value="failed">Nicht bestanden</option><option value="passed">Bestanden</option></select></label>'}<div class="dialog-actions"><button class="primary">Urteil verbindlich speichern</button></div></form>`);$('#manualForm').onsubmit=guard(async e=>{e.preventDefault();const review=pending?{steps:pending.map((s,i)=>({id:s.id,status:$('#mStatus-'+i).value,note:$('#mNote-'+i).value,evidence:$('#mEvidence-'+i).value}))}:{status:$('#mStatus').value,note:$('#mNote').value,evidence:$('#mEvidence').value};currentRun=await api('/runs/'+currentRun.id+'/manual','POST',{index,reviewer:$('#mReviewer').value,...review});$('#generic').close();await refresh();render()});return}
if(d.project||d.action==='new-project'){const p=state.library.projects.find(p=>p.id===d.project);generic(p?'Projekt bearbeiten':'Projekt hinzufügen',`<form id="projectForm"><label>Name<input id="pName" required value="${esc(p?.name)}"></label><label>ID<input id="pId" required pattern="[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,80}" value="${p?.id??'project-'+crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8)}" ${p?'readonly':''}></label><label>Absoluter lokaler Ordner<input id="pRoot" required value="${esc(p?.root)}" placeholder="E:\\MeinProjekt"></label><label>Ziel / Host / Instanz<input id="pTarget" value="${esc(p?.target)}"></label><label>Kandidatenidentität (Commit / Pakethash)<input id="pCandidate" value="${esc(p?.candidate)}"></label><label>Umgebungsnotizen<textarea id="pNotes">${esc(p?.notes)}</textarea></label><div class="dialog-actions"><button class="primary">Projekt speichern</button></div></form>`);$('#projectForm').onsubmit=guard(async e=>{e.preventDefault();const project={id:$('#pId').value,name:$('#pName').value,root:$('#pRoot').value,target:$('#pTarget').value,candidate:$('#pCandidate').value,notes:$('#pNotes').value},l=structuredClone(state.library);if(!p&&l.projects.some(p=>p.id===project.id))throw Error('Projekt-ID bereits vorhanden');l.projects=l.projects.filter(p=>p.id!==project.id);l.projects.push(project);await save(l);$('#generic').close();fileData=null});return}
switch(d.action){case'source-code':{const response=await fetch('/api/source',{headers:{'X-Cockpit-Token':state.token}});if(!response.ok)throw Error('Quelltextexport fehlgeschlagen');download('Evaluator-Inspector-komplettes-programm.txt',await response.text(),'text/plain');break}case'new':editor();break;case'run-selected':await start({testIds:[...selected],repeat:Number($('#repeat').value)});break;case'suite-selected':case'new-suite':suiteEditor();break;case'back-runs':show('runs');break;case'scan':{b.disabled=true;try{const projectId=$('#fileProject').value;fileData={...await api('/inventory?projectId='+encodeURIComponent(projectId)),projectId};fileRows()}finally{b.disabled=false}break}case'export-library':download('evaluator-inspector-library.json',JSON.stringify(state.library,null,2));break;case'import':generic('Testbibliothek importieren',`<p class="note">Import ersetzt die aktuelle Bibliothek nach Prüfung. export function renderQuick(ctx){const {state,api,adopt,show,generic,toast,esc}=ctx,$=s=>document.querySelector(s),presets=state.presets.presets;
 $('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="page-head"><div><div class="eyebrow">AUS BEWERTUNGEN WERDEN PRÜFBARE FÄLLE</div><h1>Schnellmenü</h1><p class="subtitle">8 Lehren, 35 Prüfzwecke und 65 einzeln bewertete Testfälle. Jede Vorlage erzeugt einen Testplan mit getrenntem Erfolgsfall, Gegenprobe und Wirkungsnachweis.</p></div></div><div class="toolbar"><input id="quickSearch" placeholder="Prüfzweck, Thema oder Z-ID suchen …" aria-label="Schnellmenü durchsuchen"><select id="quickGroup" aria-label="Vorlagengruppe"><option value="">Alle 108 Vorlagen</option><option>Lehren</option><option>Prüfzwecke</option><option>Einzelbewertungen</option></select></div><div class="callout"><strong>Testumgebung und Auswertung gehören zum Test.</strong>Konfiguriere Ziel, Kandidatenidentität und unabhängigen Sollwert. Manuelle Abläufe bleiben bis zum dokumentierten Urteil offen. Angeschlossene Testprogramme führen ihre echte Prüfung aus; historische Bewertungen werden nie als neue Resultate übernommen.</div><div class="suite-grid spacer" id="quickCards"></div>`;
 const draw=()=>{const q=$('#quickSearch').value.toLowerCase(),group=$('#quickGroup').value;$('#quickCards').innerHTML=presets.filter(p=>(!group||p.group===group)&&[p.id,p.name,p.purpose,p.oracle].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)).map(p=>`<article class="suite-card"><div class="eyebrow">${esc(p.id)} · ${esc(p.group)} · ${esc(p.category)}</div><h2>${esc(p.name)}</h2><p>${esc(p.purpose)}</p><details><summary class="note">Sollkriterium & Gegenfälle</summary><p><strong>Soll:</strong> ${esc(p.oracle)}</p><p><strong>Gegenprobe:</strong> ${esc(p.negative)}</p><p class="hint">${esc(p.scope)}</p></details><div class="actions"><button class="primary" data-preset="${p.id}">Prüfplan konfigurieren →</button><span class="count-label">${(p.variants?.length??1)*3} getrennte Test-IDs</span></div></article>`).join('')};draw();$('#quickSearch').oninput=draw;$('#quickGroup').onchange=draw;
 $('#quickCards').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-preset]');if(!b)return;configure(presets.find(p=>p.id===b.dataset.preset))};

export async function readApiResponse(response) {
  const data = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(data.error ?? 'Anfrage fehlgeschlagen');
    error.status = response.status;
    error.code = data.code;
    throw error;
  }
  return data;
}

export const shouldRefreshView = (view, editorOpen) =>
  !editorOpen && ['overview', 'runs', 'suites'].includes(view);

// Keep user-controlled state when result values are refreshed.
function selectionOf(node) {
  try {
    return typeof node?.selectionStart === 'number' ? [node.selectionStart,node.selectionEnd] : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

export function preserveView(document, window, render) {
  const content = document.querySelector('#content');
  const details = [...content.querySelectorAll('details')].map(node => node.open);
  const controls = [...content.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')]
    .filter(node => node.id).map(node => ({id:node.id,value:node.value,checked:node.checked}));
  const active = document.activeElement;
  const focusId = active?.id;
  const selection = selectionOf(active);
  const comparison = document.querySelector('#comparison')?.innerHTML;
  const [x,y] = [window.scrollX,window.scrollY];
  render();
  [...content.querySelectorAll('details')].forEach((node,index) => {
    if (index < details.length) node.open = details[index];
  });
  for (const control of controls) {
    const node = document.getElementById(control.id);
    if (node) { node.value = control.value; node.checked = control.checked; }
  }
  const target = focusId && document.getElementById(focusId);
  if (target) { target.focus({preventScroll:true}); if(selection)try{target.setSelectionRange(...selection)}catch{}; }
  // A prior comparison contains old result statuses; invalidate it explicitly.
  const compare = document.querySelector('#comparison');
  if (compare && comparison) {
    compare.textContent = 'Laufergebnisse aktualisiert. Vergleichslauf erneut wählen.';
    const select = document.getElementById('compareRun');if(select)select.value='';
  }
  window.scrollTo(x,y);
}

import http from 'node:http';
import {acquireLock} from './lib/service-lock.mjs';
import {sourceText} from './lib/source-bundle.mjs';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {Store,Runner,exportRun,hash} from './lib/runner.mjs';
import {extraNames} from './lib/extended-contract.mjs';
import {instantiatePreset} from './lib/quick-plans.mjs';
import {suiteVerdict} from './lib/gates.mjs';
import {seed} from './lib/seed.mjs';
import {inventory} from './lib/adapters.mjs';
import {VERSION,TYPES,CATEGORIES,templates,validateLibrary} from './lib/contract.mjs';
export const ROOT=path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export function createApp({shutdownGraceMs=1000,dataDir=process.env.COCKPIT_DATA??path.join(ROOT,'.data'),initial=seed(ROOT)}={}){
 const release=acquireLock(dataDir);let store;try{store=new Store(dataDir,initial)}catch(e){release();throw e}const runner=new Runner(store),token=crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
 const presets=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT,'resources','quick-presets.json'),'utf8'));
 const gates=()=>Object.fromEntries(store.library.suites.map(s=>[s.id,suiteVerdict(s,store.library,store.runs.values(),runner.engineHash)]));
 let boundPort;
 const server=http.createServer(async(req,res)=>{
 const send=(code,data,type='application/json')=>{res.writeHead(code,{'Content-Type':type+'; charset=utf-8','Cache-Control':'no-store','X-Content-Type-Options':'nosniff','Content-Security-Policy':"default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'"});res.end(type==='application/json'?JSON.stringify(data):data)};
 try{
 const localPort=server.address()?.port??boundPort;if(!localPort){res.destroy();return}const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${localPort}`;
 if(![`127.0.0.1:${localPort}`,`localhost:${localPort}`].includes(req.headers.host))return send(403,{error:'Ungültiger Host'});
 if(req.headers.origin&&![origin,`http://localhost:${localPort}`].includes(req.headers.origin))return send(403,{error:'Fremder Origin'});
 const url=new URL(req.url,origin),p=url.pathname;
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/health')return send(200,{ok:true,application:'evaluator-inspector',root:ROOT,version:VERSION,engineHash:runner.engineHash,pid:process.pid,dataDir:path.resolve(dataDir),processStartedAt:new Date(Date.now()-process.uptime()*1000).toISOString()});
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/bootstrap')return send(200,{token,version:VERSION,types:TYPES,typeNames:extraNames,categories:CATEGORIES,templates,library:store.library,libraryRevision:hash(store.library),gates:gates(),presets,runs:store.listRuns(),warnings:store.warnings,active:runner.active?.id??null});
 if(p.startsWith('/api/')&&req.headers['x-cockpit-token']!==token)return send(403,{error:'Lokaler Sitzungsschlüssel fehlt'});
 let body={};if(['POST','PUT'].includes(req.method)){let parts=[],size=0;for await(const b of req){size+=b.length;if(size>4*1024*1024)return send(413,{error:'Eingabe überschreitet 4 MiB'});parts.push(b)}body=JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(parts).toString()||'{}')}
 if(closing&&['POST','PUT','DELETE'].includes(req.method))return send(503,{code:'SERVICE_CLOSING',error:'Dienst wird beendet; keine neue Änderung angenommen'});
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/library')return send(200,store.library);
 if(req.method==='PUT'&&p==='/api/library'){if(runner.active)return send(409,{code:'RUN_ACTIVE',error:'Bibliothek während eines Laufs gesperrt'});validateLibrary(body);if(req.headers['if-match']!==hash(store.library))return send(409,{code:'LIBRARY_CONFLICT',error:'Bibliothek wurde inzwischen geändert. Entwurf sichern und aktuellen Stand laden.'});for(const project of body.projects){if(!fs.existsSync(project.root)||!fs.statSync(project.root).isDirectory())throw Error('Projektordner fehlt: '+project.root)}store.saveLibrary(body);return send(200,{library:store.library,libraryRevision:hash(store.library),gates:gates()})}
 if(req.method==='POST'&&p==='/api/quick-plans'){if(runner.active)throw Error('Bibliothek während eines Laufs gesperrt');if(req.headers['if-match']!==hash(store.library))return send(409,{code:'LIBRARY_CONFLICT',error:'Bibliothek inzwischen geändert. Entwurf sichern und aktuellen Stand laden.'});const preset=presets.presets.find(p=>p.id===body.presetId);if(!preset)throw Error('Vorlage fehlt');const library=structuredClone(store.library),project=library.projects.find(p=>p.id===body.projectId);if(!project)throw Error('Testumgebung fehlt');if(body.environment){for(const k of Object.keys(body.environment))if(!['target','candidate'].includes(k))throw Error('Unbekanntes Umgebungsfeld');Object.assign(project,body.environment)}const adapted={...preset};if(body.adaptation){for(const k of Object.keys(body.adaptation)){if(!['oracle','negative','scope'].includes(k)||typeof body.adaptation[k]!=='string'||!body.adaptation[k].trim())throw Error('Vorlagenanpassung ungültig');adapted[k]=body.adaptation[k]}}const plan=instantiatePreset(adapted,project,body);library.tests.push(...plan.tests);library.suites.push(plan.suite);store.saveLibrary(library);return send(201,{library:store.library,libraryRevision:hash(store.library),gates:gates(),suiteId:plan.suite.id})}
 if(req.method==='POST'&&p==='/api/shutdown'){if(runner.active)return send(409,{error:'Aktiven Lauf zuerst abbrechen und Abschluss abwarten'});send(202,{shutdownRequested:true});close().catch(()=>{});return}
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/source')return send(200,sourceText(ROOT),'text/plain');
 if(req.method==='POST'&&p==='/api/validate'){validateLibrary(body);return send(200,{valid:true})}
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/inventory'){const project=store.library.projects.find(x=>x.id===url.searchParams.get('projectId'));if(!project)throw Error('Projekt fehlt');const data=inventory(project.root);for(const f of data.files)f.testIds=store.library.tests.filter(t=>t.projectId===project.id&&['path','left','right'].some(k=>t.config[k]&&path.resolve(project.root,t.config[k])===path.resolve(project.root,f.path))).map(t=>t.id);return send(200,data)}
 if(req.method==='GET'&&p==='/api/runs')return send(200,{runs:store.listRuns(),gates:gates(),active:runner.active?.id??null});
 if(req.method==='POST'&&p==='/api/runs')return send(202,runner.start(body));
 const match=p.match(/^\/api\/runs\/([a-f0-9-]+)(?:\/(cancel|manual|export))?$/);
 if(match){const run=store.runs.get(match[1]);if(!run)return send(404,{error:'Lauf fehlt'});if(req.method==='GET'&&!match[2])return send(200,run);if(req.method==='POST'&&match[2]==='cancel'){runner.cancel(run.id);return send(202,{cancelRequested:true})}if(req.method==='POST'&&match[2]==='manual')return send(200,runner.manual(run.id,body.index,body));if(req.method==='GET'&&match[2]==='export'){const format=url.searchParams.get('format')??'json';return send(200,exportRun(run,format),format==='junit'?'application/xml':format==='csv'?'text/csv':'text/plain')}}
 if(req.method==='GET'&&['/','/app.js','/quick-menu.js','/ui-state.js','/style.css'].includes(p)){const file=p==='/'?'index.html':p.slice(1);return send(200,fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT,'public',file),'utf8'),file.endsWith('.html')?'text/html':file.endsWith('.css')?'text/css':'text/javascript')}
 send(404,{error:'Nicht gefunden'});
 }catch(e){if(res.headersSent){res.destroy();return}send(400,{error:e.message})}
 });
 let closing;
 const close=()=>closing??=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
   const timer=setTimeout(()=>server.closeAllConnections(),shutdownGraceMs);timer.unref();
   server.close(error=>{clearTimeout(timer);if(error)reject(error);else resolve()});
   server.closeIdleConnections();
 });
 server.on('listening',()=>{boundPort=server.address()?.port??boundPort});
 server.on('close',release);server.on('error',release);return {server,store,runner,close};
}
if(process.argv[1]&&path.resolve(process.argv[1])===fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)){
 const app=createApp(),port=Number(process.env.COCKPIT_PORT??4318);app.server.listen(port,'127.0.0.1',()=>console.log(`Evaluator – Inspector http://127.0.0.1:${app.server.address().port}`));app.server.on('error',e=>{console.error(e.message);process.exitCode=2});
 const stop=async()=>{if(app.runner.active){app.runner.cancel(app.runner.active.id);await app.runner.completion}await app.close()};process.on('SIGINT',stop);process.on('SIGTERM',stop);
}

import {processIsRunning} from '../lib/process-termination.mjs';
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import http from 'node:http';
import {once} from 'node:events';

import {validateLibrary,validateTest} from '../lib/contract.mjs';
import {Store,Runner,runOne,exportRun,aggregate,atomic} from '../lib/runner.mjs';
import {createApp} from '../server.mjs';
const root=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'test-cockpit-'));
const project={id:'fixture',name:'Fixture',root};
const write=(p,content)=>fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root,p),content);
const make=(type,config,extras={})=>({id:'test',name:'Fixture test',purpose:'Controlled regression evidence',type,projectId:'fixture',category:'Regression',tags:[],timeoutMs:3000,enabled:true,config,...extras});
const library=(tests)=>({schemaVersion:1,projects:[project],tests,suites:[],settings:{allowCommands:true}});
const exec=(type,c)=>execute(make(type,c),project,{allowCommands:true});
write('text.txt','Test-Cockpit\nsecond line');write('empty.txt','');write('bad-utf8.txt',Buffer.from([0xff]));
write('data.json',JSON.stringify({zero:0,no:false,empty:'',nil:null,arr:[1,2],nested:{'a/b':4}}));write('same.json','{"arr":[1,2],"nil":null,"empty":"","no":false,"zero":0,"nested":{"a/b":4}}');write('broken.json','{"bad"');
write('metrics.csv','name,value\nalpha,10\nbeta,20\n');
write('command.mjs',"console.log(process.argv.slice(2).join('|')); if(process.argv[2]==='exit-one')process.exitCode=1;");
write('slow.mjs',"import fs from 'node:fs';fs.writeFileSync(process.argv[2],String(process.pid));setInterval(()=>{},1000);");
write('tree.mjs',"import {spawn} from 'node:child_process';spawn(process.execPath,[process.argv[2],process.argv[3]],{stdio:'ignore',windowsHide:true});setInterval(()=>{},1000);");
test.after(()=>fs.rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true}));
test('contract rejects missing purpose, unknown fields/types, duplicate IDs and invalid ranges',()=>{
 const t=make('file',{path:'text.txt',exists:true});assert.equal(validateLibrary(library([t])).tests.length,1);
 for(const bad of [{...t,purpose:''},{...t,type:'magic'},{...t,config:{path:'text.txt',exists:true,typo:1}},{...t,config:{path:'text.txt',exists:true,minBytes:5,maxBytes:1}},{...t,enabled:'yes'}])assert.throws(()=>validateTest(bad,[project]));
 assert.throws(()=>validateLibrary(library([t,t])));assert.throws(()=>validateTest(make('text',{path:'text.txt'}),[project]));
});
test('file exists / absent / size / directory distinguish expected outcomes',async()=>{
 assert.equal((await exec('file',{path:'empty.txt',exists:true,minBytes:0})).status,'passed');
 assert.equal((await exec('file',{path:'empty.txt',exists:true,minBytes:1})).status,'failed');
 assert.equal((await exec('file',{path:'absent.txt',exists:false})).status,'passed');
 assert.equal((await exec('file',{path:'.',exists:true,kind:'directory'})).status,'passed');
 assert.equal((await exec('file',{path:'absent.txt',exists:true})).status,'failed');
});
test('file hash binds evidence to actual bytes',async()=>{const result=await exec('file',{path:'text.txt',exists:true,sha256:'0'.repeat(64)});assert.equal(result.status,'failed');assert.equal(result.evidence.files[0].sha256.length,64)});
test('path traversal and absolute foreign target are blocked',()=>{assert.throws(()=>scoped(root,'../foreign',true),/außerhalb/);assert.throws(()=>scoped(root,path.dirname(root),true),/außerhalb/)});
test('junction outside project is blocked and not followed by inventory',()=>{const outside=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'cockpit-outside-'));const link=path.join(root,'foreign-link');try{fs.symlinkSync(outside,link,process.platform==='win32'?'junction':'dir');assert.throws(()=>scoped(root,'foreign-link/file',true),/außerhalb/);const inv=inventory(root);assert.equal(inv.files.find(f=>f.path==='foreign-link').skipped,true)}finally{fs.unlinkSync(link);fs.rmSync(outside,{recursive:true,force:true})}});
test('text rules fail on wrong content and reject invalid UTF-8',async()=>{assert.equal((await exec('text',{path:'text.txt',includes:['second'],excludes:['ERROR']})).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('text',{path:'text.txt',includes:['missing']})).status,'failed');await assert.rejects(exec('text',{path:'bad-utf8.txt',includes:['x']}));assert.equal((await exec('text',{path:'empty.txt',maxLines:0})).status,'passed')});
test('JSON pointer preserves false, zero, null, absent and escaped keys',async()=>{assert.deepEqual(pointer({zero:0},'/zero'),{exists:true,value:0});const checks=[{path:'/zero',op:'eq',value:0},{path:'/no',op:'eq',value:false},{path:'/nil',op:'type',value:'null'},{path:'/absent',op:'exists',value:false},{path:'/empty',op:'eq',value:''},{path:'/arr/1',op:'eq',value:2},{path:'/nested/a~1b',op:'eq',value:4}];assert.equal((await exec('json',{path:'data.json',checks})).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('json',{path:'data.json',checks:[{path:'/absent',op:'ne',value:null}]})).status,'failed');await assert.rejects(exec('json',{path:'broken.json',checks}))});
test('CSV parser supports BOM, CRLF, quotes, commas, embedded newline and trailing field',()=>{assert.deepEqual(parseCSV('\uFEFFa,b,c\r\n"x,y","hello\nworld","""quoted"""\r\nx,y,\r\n'),{columns:['a','b','c'],rows:[['x,y','hello\nworld','"quoted"'],['x','y','']]});for(const s of ['a,b\n"x,y','a,b\nx','a,a\nx,y','a,b\n"x"bad,y'])assert.throws(()=>parseCSV(s))});
test('CSV checks numeric data, uniqueness and empty data without vacuous pass',async()=>{assert.equal((await exec('csv',{path:'metrics.csv',columns:['name','value'],minRows:2,unique:['name'],rules:[{column:'value',op:'gte',value:0}]})).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('csv',{path:'metrics.csv',rules:[{column:'value',op:'lt',value:15}]})).status,'failed');write('empty.csv','name,value\n');assert.equal((await exec('csv',{path:'empty.csv',rules:[{column:'value',op:'gte',value:0}]})).status,'failed')});

test('command exit and assertions are independent; arguments remain literal',async()=>{const args=['a;b','$(whoami)','x & y','space quote "','`literal`'];const c={executable:process.execPath,args:[path.join(root,'command.mjs'),...args],expectedExitCodes:[0],includes:[args.join('|')]};assert.equal((await exec('command',c)).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('command',{...c,includes:['absent']})).status,'failed');assert.equal((await exec('command',{...c,args:[path.join(root,'command.mjs'),'exit-one'],expectedExitCodes:[1],includes:['exit-one']})).status,'passed');await assert.rejects(execute(make('command',c),project,{allowCommands:false}),/deaktiviert/)});
test('worker returns error for missing executable',async()=>{const r=await runOne(make('command',{executable:path.join(root,'missing.exe'),args:[],expectedExitCodes:[0]}),project,{allowCommands:true});assert.equal(r.status,'error');assert.match(r.error,/ENOENT/)});
test('command timeout terminates process tree and records verified termination',async()=>{
 const pidFile=path.join(root,'timeout-child.pid');const t=make('command',{executable:process.execPath,args:[path.join(root,'tree.mjs'),path.join(root,'slow.mjs'),pidFile],expectedExitCodes:[0]},{timeoutMs:1200});const r=await runOne(t,project,{allowCommands:true});assert.equal(r.status,'timeout');assert.equal(r.evidence.termination,process.platform==='win32'?'process-tree-terminated':'process-group-terminated');assert.ok(fs.existsSync(pidFile));const pid=Number(fs.readFileSync(pidFile,'utf8'));assert.equal(processIsRunning(pid),false);
});
test('HTTP status, body, JSON, redirect and timeouts are distinguished',async()=>{
 const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{if(req.url==='/hang')return;if(req.url==='/redirect'){res.writeHead(302,{Location:'/redirect'});res.end();return}res.writeHead(req.url==='/missing'?404:200,{'Content-Type':'application/json'});res.end('{"ok":true}')});server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1');await once(server,'listening');const base=`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
 try{assert.equal((await exec('http',{url:base,method:'GET',status:[200],checks:[{path:'/ok',op:'eq',value:true}]})).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('http',{url:base,method:'GET',status:[200],includes:['wrong']})).status,'failed');assert.equal((await exec('http',{url:base+'/missing',method:'GET',status:[404]})).status,'passed');assert.equal((await exec('http',{url:base+'/redirect',method:'GET',status:[200]})).status,'failed');assert.equal((await runOne(make('http',{url:base+'/hang',method:'GET',status:[200]},{timeoutMs:500}),project,{})).status,'timeout')}finally{server.closeAllConnections();await new Promise(r=>server.close(r))}
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import net from 'node:net';
import {once} from 'node:events';
import {spawn,spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {createApp,ROOT} from '../server.mjs';
import {sourceFiles} from '../lib/source-bundle.mjs';
const root=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'evaluator-delivery-'));
test.after(()=>fs.rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true}));
const pwsh='C:/Program Files/PowerShell/7/pwsh.exe';

test('shutdown deadline closes an unfinished request and releases the writer lock',async()=>{
  const dir=path.join(root,'slow');const app=createApp({dataDir:dir,shutdownGraceMs:100});
  app.server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1');await once(app.server,'listening');const port=app.server.address().port;
  const socket=net.createConnection({host:'127.0.0.1',port});await once(socket,'connect');
  socket.on('error',()=>{});socket.resume();const closed=once(socket,'close');
  socket.write(`POST /api/runs HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:${port}\r\nContent-Length: 1000\r\n\r\n{`);
  await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,20));
  const start=performance.now();await app.close();await closed;
  assert.ok(performance.now()-start<2000);assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(dir,'service.lock')),false);
});

test('Windows stop script proves child process exit and lock removal',{skip:process.platform!=='win32'},async()=>{
  const dir=path.join(root,'stop');
  const child=spawn(process.execPath,[path.join(ROOT,'server.mjs')],{env:{...process.env,COCKPIT_DATA:dir,COCKPIT_PORT:'0'},stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe'],windowsHide:true});
  let output='',errors='';child.stderr.on('data',b=>errors+=b);const exited=once(child,'exit');
  try {
    const port=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(Error('start deadline: '+errors)),5000);
      child.once('error',reject);child.stdout.on('data',b=>{output+=b;const match=output.match(/127\.0\.0\.1:(\d+)/);if(match){clearTimeout(timer);resolve(match[1])}});
    });
    const stop=spawnSync(pwsh,['-NoProfile','-File',path.join(ROOT,'Stop-Evaluator.ps1'),'-Port',port],{encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true,timeout:15000});
    assert.equal(stop.status,0,stop.stderr);assert.match(stop.stdout,/Sperre freigegeben|sperre freigegeben/);
    assert.deepEqual(await exited,[0,null]);assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(dir,'service.lock')),false);
  } finally {if(child.exitCode===null)child.kill()}
});

test('packager output is read independently using .NET ZIP and SHA256',{skip:process.platform!=='win32'},()=>{
  const copy=path.join(root,'package');fs.mkdirSync(copy);
  for(const {name,data} of sourceFiles(ROOT)){const file=path.join(copy,name);fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(file,data)}
  const built=spawnSync(process.execPath,[path.join(copy,'tools/package.mjs')],{encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true});assert.equal(built.status,0,built.stderr);
  const verified=spawnSync(pwsh,['-NoProfile','-File',path.join(copy,'tools/verify-package.ps1'),'-Root',copy],{encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true});
  assert.equal(verified.status,0,verified.stderr);assert.match(verified.stdout,/PACKAGE_VERIFIED entries=\d+ sha256=verified reader=dotnet/);
});

test('one data directory has one writer and releases its lock on close',async()=>{const dir=path.join(root,'lock'),app=createApp({dataDir:dir});assert.throws(()=>createApp({dataDir:dir}),/bereits/);app.server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1');await once(app.server,'listening');await new Promise(r=>app.server.close(r));assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(dir,'service.lock')),false);const release=acquireLock(dir);release()});
test('real service shutdown and restart preserve saved definitions and evidence',async()=>{
 const dir=path.join(root,'restart');
 const launch=async()=>{const child=spawn(process.execPath,[path.join(ROOT,'server.mjs')],{cwd:ROOT,env:{...process.env,COCKPIT_DATA:dir,COCKPIT_PORT:'0'},windowsHide:true,stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe']});let out='',err='';child.stderr.on('data',b=>err+=b);const base=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>{child.kill();reject(Error('Starttimeout '+err))},10000);child.once('error',e=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(e)});child.stdout.on('data',b=>{out+=b;const m=out.match(/http:\/\/127.0.0.1:\d+/);if(m){clearTimeout(timer);resolve(m[0])}})});return {child,base}};
 const stop=async({child,base},token)=>{const exited=once(child,'exit');const r=await fetch(base+'/api/shutdown',{method:'POST',headers:{'X-Cockpit-Token':token}});assert.equal(r.status,202);const [code]=await exited;assert.equal(code,0);await assert.rejects(fetch(base+'/api/health'));};
 let instance=await launch();let b=await (await fetch(instance.base+'/api/bootstrap')).json();const headers={'Content-Type':'application/json','X-Cockpit-Token':b.token,'If-Match':b.libraryRevision};b.library.projects[0].notes='Lifecycle persistence proof';assert.equal((await fetch(instance.base+'/api/library',{method:'PUT',headers,body:JSON.stringify(b.library)})).status,200);const started=await (await fetch(instance.base+'/api/runs',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({testIds:['readme'],requestId:'restart-proof'})})).json();let run;for(let i=0;i<100;i++){run=await (await fetch(instance.base+'/api/runs/'+started.id,{headers})).json();if(run.status!=='running')break;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20))}assert.equal(run.status,'passed');await stop(instance,b.token);
 instance=await launch();b=await (await fetch(instance.base+'/api/bootstrap')).json();assert.equal(b.library.projects[0].notes,'Lifecycle persistence proof');const saved=await (await fetch(instance.base+'/api/runs/'+run.id,{headers:{'X-Cockpit-Token':b.token}})).json();assert.deepEqual(saved,run);await stop(instance,b.token);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import http from 'node:http';
import net from 'node:net';

import {evaluateExpression} from '../lib/expression.mjs';

import {createApp,ROOT} from '../server.mjs';

const root=fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'evaluator-patch-'));
test.after(()=>fs.rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true}));
const project={id:'fixture',name:'Fixture',root};
const make=(type,config,id='fixture')=>({id,name:id,purpose:'Patch regression',projectId:'fixture',type,timeoutMs:4000,config});
const library=(tests=[])=>({schemaVersion:1,projects:[project],tests,suites:[],settings:{allowCommands:true}});
const write=(name,value)=>fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root,name),value);
const exec=(type,config)=>execute(make(type,config),project,{allowCommands:true});

test('JSON pointer only resolves canonical array indices; object keys stay valid',()=>{
  const value={a:[1,2],o:{length:3,'01':'value'}};
  for(const key of ['length','01','-','-1','1e0'])assert.deepEqual(pointer(value,'/a/'+key),{exists:false});
  assert.deepEqual(pointer(value,'/a/0'),{exists:true,value:1});
  assert.deepEqual(pointer(value,'/o/length'),{exists:true,value:3});
  assert.deepEqual(pointer(value,'/o/01'),{exists:true,value:'value'});
});

test('numeric CSV rules reject invalid values even for ne',async t=>{
  for(const raw of ['Infinity','-Infinity','NaN','0x10','','1e999','bad'])await t.test(JSON.stringify(raw),async()=>{
    write('number.csv','x\n"'+raw+'"\n');
    for(const op of ['eq','ne','gte'])assert.equal((await exec('csv',{path:'number.csv',rules:[{column:'x',op,value:0}]})).status,'failed');
  });
  write('number.csv','x\n" 1e3 "\n');
  assert.equal((await exec('csv',{path:'number.csv',rules:[{column:'x',op:'eq',value:1000}]})).status,'passed');
});

test('text lines count physical lines for LF, CRLF and CR',async()=>{
  for(const [text,count] of [['',0],['a',1],['a\n',1],['\n',1],['a\n\n',2],['a\rb',2],['a\r\nb\r\n',2]]){
    write('lines.txt',text);const result=await exec('text',{path:'lines.txt',minLines:count,maxLines:count});
    assert.equal(result.status,'passed',JSON.stringify(text));assert.equal(result.evidence.lines,count);
  }
});

test('text comparison normalizes CR but byte comparison preserves it',async()=>{
  write('left.txt','a\rb\r');write('right.txt','a\nb\n');
  assert.equal((await exec('compare',{left:'left.txt',right:'right.txt',mode:'text'})).status,'passed');
  assert.equal((await exec('compare',{left:'left.txt',right:'right.txt',mode:'bytes'})).status,'failed');
});

test('command output preserves split UTF-8 on both streams',async()=>{
  write('split.mjs',`process.stdout.write(Buffer.from([0xc3]));process.stderr.write(Buffer.from([0xf0,0x9f]));setTimeout(()=>{process.stdout.write(Buffer.from([0xa4]));process.stderr.write(Buffer.from([0x91,0x8d]));},80);`);
  const result=await runOne(make('command',{executable:process.execPath,args:[path.join(root,'split.mjs')],expectedExitCodes:[0],includes:['ä'],excludes:['�']}),project,{allowCommands:true});
  assert.equal(result.status,'passed');assert.equal(result.evidence.process.stdout,'ä');assert.equal(result.evidence.process.stderr,'👍');
});

test('invalid UTF-8 process output is a controlled adapter error',async()=>{
  write('invalid.mjs','process.stdout.write(Buffer.from([0xff]));');
  const result=await runOne(make('command',{executable:process.execPath,args:[path.join(root,'invalid.mjs')],expectedExitCodes:[0]}),project,{allowCommands:true});

});

test('unverified termination overrides a successful result',async()=>{
  write('exists.txt','x');
  const result=await runOne(make('file',{path:'exists.txt',exists:true}),project,{},undefined,{terminate:async child=>{await killTree(child);throw Error('injected verification failure')}});
  assert.equal(result.status,'error');assert.equal(result.evidence.termination,'unverified');assert.equal(result.evidence.requestedStatus,'passed');
});

test('CSV export neutralizes formulas behind whitespace without breaking quoting',()=>{
  for(const value of ['=1','\t=1','\r=1',' =1','\n@SUM(1)','\ttext','+1','-1']){
    const exported=exportRun({results:[{testId:'id',status:'error',error:value}]},'csv');
    assert.equal(parseCSV(exported).rows[0][4],"'"+value);
  }
  assert.equal(parseCSV(exportRun({results:[{testId:'id',status:'error',error:'safe,"text"'}]},'csv')).rows[0][4],'safe,"text"');
});

test('raw text and RCDATA cannot manufacture HTML elements or event attributes',()=>{
  for(const tag of ['script','style','textarea','title']){
    const input=`<${tag}><button onclick="bad()">fake</button></${tag}><button>real</button>`;
    const nodes=parseHTML(input).nodes;
    assert.deepEqual(nodes.map(n=>n.tag),[tag,'button']);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(parseHTML('<script></scripture><button>fake</button></SCRIPT ><button>real</button>').nodes.map(n=>n.tag),['script','button']);
  assert.equal(checkHTML('<button><script>fake name</script></button>',['button-name'],{})[0].passed,false);
});

test('accessible image names respect alt and aria-hidden',()=>{
  for(const html of ['<button><img alt="Search"></button>','<input type="image" alt="Search">','<button><svg><title>Search</title></svg></button>']){
    assert.ok(checkHTML(html,['button-name','input-name'],{}).every(r=>r.passed),html);
  }
  for(const html of ['<button><img alt=""></button>','<button><img alt="Search" aria-hidden="true"></button>'])assert.equal(checkHTML(html,['button-name'],{})[0].passed,false);
});

  assert.equal(evaluateExpression('len("👍")'),1);assert.equal(evaluateExpression('len("a👍")'),2);
  assert.equal(evaluateExpression('len("é")'),2);assert.equal(evaluateExpression('len([1,2])'),2);
});

test('recovery skips corrupt newest backup and preserves original bytes',()=>{
  const dir=path.join(root,'recovery');const store=new Store(dir,library());
  const backups=path.join(dir,'backups');fs.mkdirSync(backups);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(backups,'1000-aaaa.json'),JSON.stringify(store.library));
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(backups,'2000-bbbb.json'),'{bad backup');
  fs.writeFileSync(store.libraryFile,Buffer.from([0xff,0x00,0x7b]));
  const recovered=new Store(dir,library());
  assert.equal(recovered.library.projects[0].id,'fixture');assert.equal(recovered.library.settings.allowCommands,false);
  assert.equal(recovered.warnings.length,2);
  const preserved=fs.readdirSync(dir).find(n=>n.startsWith('library.corrupt-'));
  assert.deepEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,preserved)),Buffer.from([0xff,0x00,0x7b]));
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(store.libraryFile,'utf8')),recovered.library);
});

    const run=runner.start({testIds:['first','second'],stopOnFailure});await runner.completion;

  }
